- ASTRA-2026-09-14-T2 | plans/ASTRA-2026-09-14-derivation.md:28 | For every real number field `F`, `E = F(i)`, `R` its unit-vector ring, and every rational prime `p`: `1/p ∈ R` iff no conjugation-stable prime of `E` lies above `p`.
